Abstract: This paper introduces temporal-conditioned normalizing flows (tcNF), a novel framework that addresses anomaly detection in time series data with accurate modeling of temporal dependencies and uncertainty. By conditioning normalizing flows on previous observations, tcNF effectively captures complex temporal dynamics and generates accurate probability distributions of expected behavior. This autoregressive approach enables robust anomaly detection by identifying low-probability events within the learned distribution. We evaluate tcNF on diverse datasets, demonstrating good accuracy and robustness compared to existing methods. A comprehensive analysis of strengths and limitations and open-source code is provided to facilitate reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [118] [Efficiently Aligning Draft Models via Parameter- and Data-Efficient Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09527)
*Luxi Lin,Zhihang Lin,Zhanpeng Zeng,Yuhao Chen,Qingyu Zhang,Jixiang Luo,Xuelong Li,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EDA框架通过解耦架构、数据再生和样本选择机制，高效适配草稿模型以恢复微调后目标模型的推测解码性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码在LLM推理中能加速，但当目标模型针对特定领域微调时性能会下降。重新训练草稿模型成本高且低效，需要参数和数据高效的适配方案

Method: 提出EDA框架：1）解耦架构使用共享和私有组件分别建模共享和目标特定的输出分布，仅更新轻量级私有组件；2）数据再生策略利用微调的目标模型生成训练数据，提高训练与推测解码的对齐；3）样本选择机制优先选择高价值数据进行高效适配

Result: 实验表明EDA能有效恢复微调模型上的推测解码性能，以显著降低的训练成本实现更优的平均接受长度

Conclusion: EDA为推测解码在微调模型上的性能恢复提供了参数和数据高效的解决方案，代码已开源

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference but suffers from performance degradation when target models are fine-tuned for specific domains. A naive solution is to retrain draft models for every target model, which is costly and inefficient. To address this, we introduce a parameter- and data-efficient framework named Efficient Draft Adaptation, abbreviated as EDA, for efficiently adapting draft models. EDA introduces three innovations: (1) a decoupled architecture that utilizes shared and private components to model the shared and target-specific output distributions separately, enabling parameter-efficient adaptation by updating only the lightweight private component;(2) a data regeneration strategy that utilizes the fine-tuned target model to regenerate training data, thereby improving the alignment between training and speculative decoding, leading to higher average acceptance length;(3) a sample selection mechanism that prioritizes high-value data for efficient adaptation. Our experiments show that EDA effectively restores speculative performance on fine-tuned models, achieving superior average acceptance lengths with significantly reduced training costs compared to full retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/Lyn-Lucy/Efficient-Draft-Adaptation.

</details>


### [119] [Towards Understanding Adam Convergence on Highly Degenerate Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09581)
*Zhiwei Bai,Jiajie Zhao,Zhangchen Zhou,Zhi-Qin John Xu,Yaoyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Adam优化算法在高度退化多项式上的自然自动收敛特性，证明了Adam在此类函数上具有局部线性收敛速度，显著优于梯度下降和动量法的次线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam是深度学习中被广泛使用的优化算法，但其在哪些目标函数上具有固有优势仍不明确。先前研究需要外部调度器和β₂接近1才能保证收敛，而本文旨在探索Adam的"自然"自动收敛特性。

Method: 研究了一类高度退化多项式，推导了Adam在此类函数上局部渐近稳定的理论条件。分析了Adam中二阶矩v_t与梯度平方g_t²之间的解耦机制，该机制能指数级放大有效学习率。还刻画了Adam的超参数相图，识别了三种不同的行为模式。

Result: 理论证明Adam在退化多项式上能实现局部线性收敛，显著优于梯度下降和动量法的次线性收敛。实验结果表明理论边界与实验结果高度一致。识别了Adam的三个行为模式：稳定收敛、尖峰和SignGD类振荡。

Conclusion: Adam在一类高度退化多项式上具有固有的自动收敛优势，其加速源于二阶矩与梯度平方的解耦机制。该研究为理解Adam的收敛特性提供了新的理论框架，并揭示了其超参数相图中的不同行为模式。

Abstract: Adam is a widely used optimization algorithm in deep learning, yet the specific class of objective functions where it exhibits inherent advantages remains underexplored. Unlike prior studies requiring external schedulers and $β_2$ near 1 for convergence, this work investigates the "natural" auto-convergence properties of Adam. We identify a class of highly degenerate polynomials where Adam converges automatically without additional schedulers. Specifically, we derive theoretical conditions for local asymptotic stability on degenerate polynomials and demonstrate strong alignment between theoretical bounds and experimental results. We prove that Adam achieves local linear convergence on these degenerate functions, significantly outperforming the sub-linear convergence of Gradient Descent and Momentum. This acceleration stems from a decoupling mechanism between the second moment $v_t$ and squared gradient $g_t^2$, which exponentially amplifies the effective learning rate. Finally, we characterize Adam's hyperparameter phase diagram, identifying three distinct behavioral regimes: stable convergence, spikes, and SignGD-like oscillation.

</details>


### [120] [Nonparametric Variational Differential Privacy via Embedding Parameter Clipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09583)
*Dina El Zein,Shashi Kumar,James Henderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种参数裁剪策略来改善非参数变分信息瓶颈中的隐私-效用权衡，通过数学推导的约束条件防止潜在表示漂移，在增强隐私保护的同时提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非参数变分信息瓶颈(NVIB)是非参数变分差分隐私(NVDP)的基础，但学习到的潜在表示可能漂移到高信息区域，导致隐私保护效果差且训练时数值不稳定，从而降低模型效用。

Method: 提出基于数学推导的参数裁剪策略，通过最小化Rényi散度上界的目标函数，推导出对后验均值、方差和混合权重参数的具体理论约束，应用于NVIB模型。

Result: 裁剪后的模型相比无约束基线，在多个下游任务上获得更高性能，同时实现更紧的Rényi散度上界，意味着更强的隐私保护，改善了隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 该方法为变分模型提供了一种简单有效的改进隐私-效用权衡的方法，使其更加鲁棒和实用，解决了潜在表示漂移导致的隐私和数值稳定性问题。

Abstract: The nonparametric variational information bottleneck (NVIB) provides the foundation for nonparametric variational differential privacy (NVDP), a framework for building privacy-preserving language models. However, the learned latent representations can drift into regions with high information content, leading to poor privacy guarantees, but also low utility due to numerical instability during training. In this work, we introduce a principled parameter clipping strategy to directly address this issue. Our method is mathematically derived from the objective of minimizing the Rényi Divergence (RD) upper bound, yielding specific, theoretically grounded constraints on the posterior mean, variance, and mixture weight parameters. We apply our technique to an NVIB based model and empirically compare it against an unconstrained baseline. Our findings demonstrate that the clipped model consistently achieves tighter RD bounds, implying stronger privacy, while simultaneously attaining higher performance on several downstream tasks. This work presents a simple yet effective method for improving the privacy-utility trade-off in variational models, making them more robust and practical.

</details>


### [121] [MM-algorithms for traditional and convex NMF with Tweedie and Negative Binomial cost functions and empirical evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09601)
*Elisabeth Sommer James,Asger Hobolth,Marta Pelizzola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个统一的非负矩阵分解框架，支持多种噪声分布假设，包括负二项分布和Tweedie模型，并推导了相应的乘法更新规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NMF方法通常基于高斯或泊松噪声假设，无法有效处理具有过度离散或复杂均值-方差关系的数据。需要建立一个更通用的框架来适应多种分布假设。

Method: 使用Majorize-Minimisation方法，推导了所有考虑模型的乘法更新规则，包括泊松和负二项成本函数的凸NMF新更新规则。提供了统一实现，包括多个凸NMF模型的首次实现。

Result: 在突变和词频数据上的实证评估表明，噪声模型的选择显著影响模型拟合和特征恢复效果，凸NMF在处理类别数量大的场景中能提供高效且稳健的替代方案。

Conclusion: 提出了一个统一的NMF框架，支持多种分布假设，强调了噪声模型选择的重要性，并展示了凸NMF在大类别场景中的优势。相关代码已在R包nmfgenr中开源。

Abstract: Non-negative matrix factorisation (NMF) is a widely used tool for unsupervised learning and feature extraction, with applications ranging from genomics to text analysis and signal processing. Standard formulations of NMF are typically derived under Gaussian or Poisson noise assumptions, which may be inadequate for data exhibiting overdispersion or other complex mean-variance relationships. In this paper, we develop a unified framework for both traditional and convex NMF under a broad class of distributional assumptions, including Negative Binomial and Tweedie models, where the connection between the Tweedie and the $β$-divergence is also highlighted. Using a Majorize-Minimisation approach, we derive multiplicative update rules for all considered models, and novel updates for convex NMF with Poisson and Negative Binomial cost functions. We provide a unified implementation of all considered models, including the first implementations of several convex NMF models. Empirical evaluations on mutational and word count data demonstrate that the choice of noise model critically affects model fit and feature recovery, and that convex NMF can provide an efficient and robust alternative to traditional NMF in scenarios where the number of classes is large. The code for our proposed updates is available in the R package nmfgenr and can be found at https://github.com/MartaPelizzola/nmfgenr.

</details>


### [122] [FreqCycle: A Multi-Scale Time-Frequency Analysis Method for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09661)
*Boya Zhang,Shuaijie Yin,Huiwen Zhu,Xing He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FreqCycle是一个新颖的时间序列预测框架，通过时域周期模式学习和频域模式增强来挖掘全频段特征，解决现有方法过度关注低频而忽视中高频的问题，并在多周期耦合场景下扩展为MFreqCycle。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测研究主要集中于建模低频模式（大部分能量集中于此），而忽视中高频特征，这限制了深度学习模型的性能进一步提升。同时，时间序列数据常呈现耦合的多周期性（如交织的周周期和日周期），以及长回溯窗口的挑战。

Method: 提出FreqCycle框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 滤波增强循环预测模块（FECF），通过显式学习时域共享周期模式来提取低频特征；(2) 分段频域模式学习模块（SFPL），通过可学习滤波器和自适应加权增强中高频能量比例。针对多周期耦合问题，进一步扩展为MFreqCycle，通过跨尺度交互解耦嵌套周期特征。

Result: 在七个不同领域的基准测试上进行广泛实验，FreqCycle实现了最先进的预测精度，同时保持了更快的推理速度，在性能与效率之间达到了最佳平衡。

Conclusion: FreqCycle通过同时挖掘低频和中高频特征，有效解决了现有时间序列预测方法的局限性。其分层扩展MFreqCycle进一步解决了多周期耦合和长回溯窗口的挑战，为时间序列预测提供了性能与效率兼顾的解决方案。

Abstract: Mining time-frequency features is critical for time series forecasting. Existing research has predominantly focused on modeling low-frequency patterns, where most time series energy is concentrated. The overlooking of mid to high frequency continues to limit further performance gains in deep learning models. We propose FreqCycle, a novel framework integrating: (i) a Filter-Enhanced Cycle Forecasting (FECF) module to extract low-frequency features by explicitly learning shared periodic patterns in the time domain, and (ii) a Segmented Frequency-domain Pattern Learning (SFPL) module to enhance mid to high frequency energy proportion via learnable filters and adaptive weighting. Furthermore, time series data often exhibit coupled multi-periodicity, such as intertwined weekly and daily cycles. To address coupled multi-periodicity as well as long lookback window challenges, we extend FreqCycle hierarchically into MFreqCycle, which decouples nested periodic features through cross-scale interactions. Extensive experiments on seven diverse domain benchmarks demonstrate that FreqCycle achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while maintaining faster inference speeds, striking an optimal balance between performance and efficiency.

</details>


### [123] [No evaluation without fair representation : Impact of label and selection bias on the evaluation, performance and mitigation of classification models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09662)
*Magali Legast,Toon Calders,François Fouss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究分析了机器学习数据集中标签偏差和选择偏差对分类模型评估、性能及偏差缓解方法有效性的影响，提出了一个偏差引入和评估框架，发现偏差类型显著影响模型性能，且在无偏差测试集上不存在公平性与准确性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习数据集中的偏差（如选择偏差和标签偏差）对公平机器学习有重要影响，但不同类型偏差的不同影响尚未得到充分研究。需要系统分析这些偏差类型对模型评估、性能以及偏差缓解方法有效性的影响。

Method: 提出了一个偏差引入和评估框架，通过在低歧视的真实数据集中引入受控偏差来模拟公平世界及其有偏差的对应版本。独立分析每种偏差类型的影响，并使用无偏差测试集进行更代表性的模型评估，而非传统上使用有偏差数据子集作为测试集的方法。

Result: 发现不同因素影响偏差对模型性能的影响程度；当在无偏差测试集上评估时，不存在公平性与准确性之间的权衡，也不存在个体公平性与群体公平性之间的权衡；偏差缓解方法的性能受数据中存在的偏差类型影响。

Conclusion: 研究结果呼吁未来工作开发更准确的预测模型和公平干预评估方法，更好地理解其他偏差类型、更复杂的多偏差类型组合场景，以及影响缓解方法效率的其他因素（如数据集特征）。

Abstract: Bias can be introduced in diverse ways in machine learning datasets, for example via selection or label bias. Although these bias types in themselves have an influence on important aspects of fair machine learning, their different impact has been understudied. In this work, we empirically analyze the effect of label bias and several subtypes of selection bias on the evaluation of classification models, on their performance, and on the effectiveness of bias mitigation methods. We also introduce a biasing and evaluation framework that allows to model fair worlds and their biased counterparts through the introduction of controlled bias in real-life datasets with low discrimination. Using our framework, we empirically analyze the impact of each bias type independently, while obtaining a more representative evaluation of models and mitigation methods than with the traditional use of a subset of biased data as test set. Our results highlight different factors that influence how impactful bias is on model performance. They also show an absence of trade-off between fairness and accuracy, and between individual and group fairness, when models are evaluated on a test set that does not exhibit unwanted bias. They furthermore indicate that the performance of bias mitigation methods is influenced by the type of bias present in the data. Our findings call for future work to develop more accurate evaluations of prediction models and fairness interventions, but also to better understand other types of bias, more complex scenarios involving the combination of different bias types, and other factors that impact the efficiency of the mitigation methods, such as dataset characteristics.

</details>


### [124] [On Catastrophic Forgetting in Low-Rank Decomposition-Based Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09684)
*Muhammad Ahmad,Jingjing Zheng,Yankai Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRA等低秩分解PEFT方法在顺序学习中存在灾难性遗忘问题，研究发现遗忘程度与更新子空间的几何结构和参数化方式密切相关


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究参数高效微调（PEFT）方法（如LoRA）在顺序学习中的行为，特别是灾难性遗忘问题，目前对此理解不足

Method: 实证研究，分析更新子空间的几何结构和参数化对遗忘的影响，比较不同方法（如LoRETTA、WeGeFT）的表现

Result: 遗忘程度受更新子空间几何结构和参数化影响显著：限制在小共享矩阵子空间的方法易受任务干扰，张量分解方法（LoRETTA）在超紧凑预算下捕获更丰富结构信息从而减轻遗忘，结构对齐参数化（WeGeFT）能保持预训练表示

Conclusion: 更新子空间设计是持续学习的关键因素，为顺序设置中选择高效适应策略提供实用指导

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) based on low-rank decomposition, such as LoRA, has become a standard for adapting large pretrained models. However, its behavior in sequential learning -- specifically regarding catastrophic forgetting -- remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we present an empirical study showing that forgetting is strongly influenced by the geometry and parameterization of the update subspace. While methods that restrict updates to small, shared matrix subspaces often suffer from task interference, tensor-based decompositions (e.g., LoRETTA) mitigate forgetting by capturing richer structural information within ultra-compact budgets, and structurally aligned parameterizations (e.g., WeGeFT) preserve pretrained representations. Our findings highlight update subspace design as a key factor in continual learning and offer practical guidance for selecting efficient adaptation strategies in sequential settings.

</details>


### [125] [Physics-informed neural operator for predictive parametric phase-field modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09693)
*Nanxi Chen,Airong Chen,Rujin Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PF-PINO：一种物理信息神经算子框架，用于学习参数化相场偏微分方程，通过嵌入物理约束显著提升精度、泛化能力和长期稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场建模用于预测材料微观结构和形态演化，计算成本高昂，尤其在高通量参数研究中。传统神经算子（如傅里叶神经算子FNO）缺乏显式物理约束，可能限制复杂相场动力学的泛化能力和长期精度。

Method: 开发PF-PINO物理信息神经算子框架，将相场控制方程的残差嵌入数据保真度损失函数中，在训练过程中有效强制执行物理约束。该方法结合了神经算子的参数化PDE求解能力和物理约束的嵌入。

Result: PF-PINO在电化学腐蚀、枝晶凝固和旋节分解等基准相场问题上验证，显著优于传统FNO，在精度、泛化能力和长期稳定性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: PF-PINO为相场建模提供了稳健高效的计算工具，展示了物理信息神经算子在推进复杂界面演化问题的科学机器学习方面的潜力。

Abstract: Predicting the microstructural and morphological evolution of materials through phase-field modelling is computationally intensive, particularly for high-throughput parametric studies. While neural operators such as the Fourier neural operator (FNO) show promise in accelerating the solution of parametric partial differential equations (PDEs), the lack of explicit physical constraints, may limit generalisation and long-term accuracy for complex phase-field dynamics. Here, we develop a physics-informed neural operator framework to learn parametric phase-field PDEs, namely PF-PINO. By embedding the residuals of phase-field governing equations into the data-fidelity loss function, our framework effectively enforces physical constraints during training. We validate PF-PINO against benchmark phase-field problems, including electrochemical corrosion, dendritic crystal solidification, and spinodal decomposition. Our results demonstrate that PF-PINO significantly outperforms conventional FNO in accuracy, generalisation capability, and long-term stability. This work provides a robust and efficient computational tool for phase-field modelling and highlights the potential of physics-informed neural operators to advance scientific machine learning for complex interfacial evolution problems.

</details>


### [126] [A Multi-Prototype-Guided Federated Knowledge Distillation Approach in AI-RAN Enabled Multi-Access Edge Computing System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09727)
*Luyao Zou,Hayoung Oh,Chu Myaet Thwal,Apurba Adhikary,Seohyeon Hong,Zhu Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MP-FedKD方法，通过多原型引导的联邦知识蒸馏解决非IID数据下的联邦学习问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI-RAN与MEC融合的系统中，联邦学习面临非独立同分布数据的挑战，传统单原型平均方法会导致信息损失

Method: 提出MP-FedKD方法：集成自知识蒸馏处理非IID问题；采用多原型策略，包括条件层次凝聚聚类和原型对齐方案；设计LEMGP损失函数关注全局原型与局部嵌入关系

Result: 在多个数据集和不同非IID设置下的实验表明，MP-FedKD在准确率、平均准确率以及RMSE和MAE误差方面优于现有基线方法

Conclusion: MP-FedKD通过多原型策略有效解决了联邦学习中非IID数据带来的信息损失问题，提升了AI-RAN使能MEC系统的性能

Abstract: With the development of wireless network, Multi-Access Edge Computing (MEC) and Artificial Intelligence (AI)-native Radio Access Network (RAN) have attracted significant attention. Particularly, the integration of AI-RAN and MEC is envisioned to transform network efficiency and responsiveness. Therefore, it is valuable to investigate AI-RAN enabled MEC system. Federated learning (FL) nowadays is emerging as a promising approach for AI-RAN enabled MEC system, in which edge devices are enabled to train a global model cooperatively without revealing their raw data. However, conventional FL encounters the challenge in processing the non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data. Single prototype obtained by averaging the embedding vectors per class can be employed in FL to handle the data heterogeneity issue. Nevertheless, this may result in the loss of useful information owing to the average operation. Therefore, in this paper, a multi-prototype-guided federated knowledge distillation (MP-FedKD) approach is proposed. Particularly, self-knowledge distillation is integrated into FL to deal with the non-IID issue. To cope with the problem of information loss caused by single prototype-based strategy, multi-prototype strategy is adopted, where we present a conditional hierarchical agglomerative clustering (CHAC) approach and a prototype alignment scheme. Additionally, we design a novel loss function (called LEMGP loss) for each local client, where the relationship between global prototypes and local embedding will be focused. Extensive experiments over multiple datasets with various non-IID settings showcase that the proposed MP-FedKD approach outperforms the considered state-of-the-art baselines regarding accuracy, average accuracy and errors (RMSE and MAE).

</details>


### [127] [Exploiting Label-Aware Channel Scoring for Adaptive Channel Pruning in Split Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09792)
*Jialei Tan,Zheng Lin,Xiangming Cai,Ruoxi Zhu,Zihan Fang,Pingping Chen,Wei Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ACP-SL方案，通过自适应通道剪枝减少分割学习中的通信开销，同时保持模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分割学习将大部分训练工作负载转移到服务器，减轻了客户端设备的计算负担，但中间特征表示（称为粉碎数据）的传输会产生显著的通信开销，特别是在涉及大量客户端设备时

Method: 提出自适应通道剪枝辅助的分割学习方案（ACP-SL），包含标签感知通道重要性评分模块（LCIS）和自适应通道剪枝模块（ACP）。LCIS模块生成通道重要性分数，区分重要通道和不重要通道；ACP模块基于这些分数剪枝不重要通道，压缩对应的粉碎数据

Result: 实验结果表明ACP-SL在测试准确率上始终优于基准方案，并且能以更少的训练轮数达到目标测试准确率，从而减少通信开销

Conclusion: ACP-SL方案通过自适应通道剪枝有效减少了分割学习中的通信开销，同时保持了模型性能，实现了计算负担和通信开销之间的良好平衡

Abstract: Split learning (SL) transfers most of the training workload to the server, which alleviates computational burden on client devices. However, the transmission of intermediate feature representations, referred to as smashed data, incurs significant communication overhead, particularly when a large number of client devices are involved. To address this challenge, we propose an adaptive channel pruning-aided SL (ACP-SL) scheme. In ACP-SL, a label-aware channel importance scoring (LCIS) module is designed to generate channel importance scores, distinguishing important channels from less important ones. Based on these scores, an adaptive channel pruning (ACP) module is developed to prune less important channels, thereby compressing the corresponding smashed data and reducing the communication overhead. Experimental results show that ACP-SL consistently outperforms benchmark schemes in test accuracy. Furthermore, it reaches a target test accuracy in fewer training rounds, thereby reducing communication overhead.

</details>


### [128] [Information Theoretic Bayesian Optimization over the Probability Simplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09793)
*Federico Pavesi,Antonio Candelieri,Noémie Jaquier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出α-GaBO，一种基于信息几何的概率单纯形贝叶斯优化方法，通过构造反映单纯形几何结构的Matérn核和几何优化器，在概率混合等应用中优于传统欧几里得约束方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多应用涉及优化概率和混合问题，这些自然属于概率单纯形（非负元素和为1的约束非欧几里得域）。传统贝叶斯优化方法未充分考虑单纯形的几何结构，需要专门针对概率单纯形的优化方法。

Method: 基于信息几何理论，将概率单纯形视为黎曼流形，构造反映单纯形几何结构的Matérn核函数，并开发单参数族的几何采集函数优化器。该方法称为α-GaBO。

Result: 在基准函数和实际应用（包括组件混合、分类器混合和机器人控制任务）上验证了方法的有效性，相比约束欧几里得方法表现出更好的性能。

Conclusion: α-GaBO为概率单纯形上的贝叶斯优化提供了有效的几何框架，通过考虑域的内在几何结构，在概率混合等应用中实现了更优的性能。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization is a data-efficient technique that has been shown to be extremely powerful to optimize expensive, black-box, and possibly noisy objective functions. Many applications involve optimizing probabilities and mixtures which naturally belong to the probability simplex, a constrained non-Euclidean domain defined by non-negative entries summing to one. This paper introduces $α$-GaBO, a novel family of Bayesian optimization algorithms over the probability simplex. Our approach is grounded in information geometry, a branch of Riemannian geometry which endows the simplex with a Riemannian metric and a class of connections. Based on information geometry theory, we construct Matérn kernels that reflect the geometry of the probability simplex, as well as a one-parameter family of geometric optimizers for the acquisition function. We validate our method on benchmark functions and on a variety of real-world applications including mixtures of components, mixtures of classifiers, and a robotic control task, showing its increased performance compared to constrained Euclidean approaches.

</details>


### [129] [Good Reasoning Makes Good Demonstrations: Implicit Reasoning Quality Supervision via In-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09803)
*Tiehua Mei,Minxuan Lv,Leiyu Pan,Zhenpeng Su,Hongru Hou,Hengrui Chen,Ao Xu,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出In-Context RLVR方法，利用语言模型自身的上下文学习能力评估推理质量（Evidence Gain），在强化学习中为高质量推理轨迹分配更高权重，提升数学推理的准确性和推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RLVR将所有正确解决方案同等对待，可能强化那些偶然获得正确答案但推理过程有缺陷的轨迹。观察到高质量推理是更好的教学示范，需要一种方法来区分推理质量并相应调整强化学习权重。

Method: 提出In-Context RLVR框架：1）定义Demonstration Utility（示范效用）概念；2）利用策略模型自身的上下文学习能力测量Evidence Gain（证据增益）作为质量信号；3）通过贝叶斯分析将该信号隐式融入奖励重加权，为高质量轨迹分配更高权重，无需额外计算或外部评估器。

Result: 在数学基准测试中，相比标准RLVR方法，In-Context RLVR在准确性和推理质量方面均取得改进。

Conclusion: 利用语言模型自身的上下文学习能力评估推理质量是有效的，Evidence Gain信号能够隐式重加权强化学习奖励，提升数学推理性能，为改进强化学习在推理任务中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) improves reasoning in large language models but treats all correct solutions equally, potentially reinforcing flawed traces that get correct answers by chance. We observe that better reasoning are better teachers: high-quality solutions serve as more effective demonstrations than low-quality ones. We term this teaching ability Demonstration Utility, and show that the policy model's own in-context learning ability provides an efficient way to measure it, yielding a quality signal termed Evidence Gain. To employ this signal during training, we introduce In-Context RLVR. By Bayesian analysis, we show that this objective implicitly reweights rewards by Evidence Gain, assigning higher weights to high-quality traces and lower weights to low-quality ones, without requiring costly computation or external evaluators. Experiments on mathematical benchmarks show improvements in both accuracy and reasoning quality over standard RLVR.

</details>


### [130] [GAST: Gradient-aligned Sparse Tuning of Large Language Models with Data-layer Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09865)
*Kai Yao,Zhenghan Song,Kaixin Wu,Mingjie Zhong,Danzhao Cheng,Zhaorui Tan,Yixin Ji,Penglei Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAST是一种参数高效微调方法，通过同时在数据和层维度进行选择性微调，自适应地为每层选择最具影响力的数据点，减少冗余信息并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法通常关注两个独立范式：层选择性方法（微调关键层以减少计算负载）和数据选择性方法（选择有效训练子集以提升训练）。但这些方法忽略了不同数据点对不同模型层的贡献程度不同，且往往丢弃了被视为低质量数据的潜在有价值信息。

Method: 提出梯度对齐稀疏调优（GAST），这是一种创新方法，同时将数据和层维度的选择性微调作为统一优化策略的组成部分。GAST采用层稀疏策略，自适应地为每层选择最具影响力的数据点，针对信息冗余问题提供比单维度方法更全面和复杂的解决方案。

Result: 实验表明，GAST在性能上持续优于基线方法，为参数高效微调策略的未来研究确立了有前景的方向。

Conclusion: GAST通过同时在数据和层维度进行选择性微调，提供了一种更全面和复杂的参数高效微调解决方案，能够有效处理不同数据点对不同模型层的差异化贡献，避免丢弃潜在有价值信息，在性能上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) has become a key strategy for adapting large language models, with recent advances in sparse tuning reducing overhead by selectively updating key parameters or subsets of data. Existing approaches generally focus on two distinct paradigms: layer-selective methods aiming to fine-tune critical layers to minimize computational load, and data-selective methods aiming to select effective training subsets to boost training. However, current methods typically overlook the fact that different data points contribute varying degrees to distinct model layers, and they often discard potentially valuable information from data perceived as of low quality. To address these limitations, we propose Gradient-aligned Sparse Tuning (GAST), an innovative method that simultaneously performs selective fine-tuning at both data and layer dimensions as integral components of a unified optimization strategy. GAST specifically targets redundancy in information by employing a layer-sparse strategy that adaptively selects the most impactful data points for each layer, providing a more comprehensive and sophisticated solution than approaches restricted to a single dimension. Experiments demonstrate that GAST consistently outperforms baseline methods, establishing a promising direction for future research in PEFT strategies.

</details>


### [131] [CarbonBench: A Global Benchmark for Upscaling of Carbon Fluxes Using Zero-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09868)
*Aleksei Rozanov,Arvind Renganathan,Yimeng Zhang,Vipin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CarbonBench：首个用于碳通量升尺度零样本空间迁移学习的基准，包含全球567个通量塔站点的130万日观测数据，提供分层评估协议和标准化特征集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确量化陆地碳交换对气候政策和碳核算至关重要，但现有模型需要泛化到涡动协方差观测稀疏的生态系统。当前缺乏标准化基准来严格评估模型在不同地理位置、气候制度和植被类型下的性能。

Method: 创建CarbonBench基准，包含全球567个通量塔站点（2000-2024年）超过130万日观测数据。提供：1）分层评估协议，明确测试未见植被类型和气候制度的泛化能力；2）统一的遥感与气象特征集；3）从树基方法到领域泛化架构的基线模型。

Result: 建立了首个用于碳通量升尺度零样本空间迁移学习的标准化基准，为系统比较迁移学习方法提供了平台，同时作为分布偏移下回归问题的测试平台。

Conclusion: CarbonBench通过连接机器学习方法与地球系统科学，旨在促进迁移学习方法的系统比较，为分布偏移下的回归提供测试平台，并支持下一代气候建模工作。

Abstract: Accurately quantifying terrestrial carbon exchange is essential for climate policy and carbon accounting, yet models must generalize to ecosystems underrepresented in sparse eddy covariance observations. Despite this challenge being a natural instance of zero-shot spatial transfer learning for time series regression, no standardized benchmark exists to rigorously evaluate model performance across geographically distinct locations with different climate regimes and vegetation types.
  We introduce CarbonBench, the first benchmark for zero-shot spatial transfer in carbon flux upscaling. CarbonBench comprises over 1.3 million daily observations from 567 flux tower sites globally (2000-2024). It provides: (1) stratified evaluation protocols that explicitly test generalization across unseen vegetation types and climate regimes, separating spatial transfer from temporal autocorrelation; (2) a harmonized set of remote sensing and meteorological features to enable flexible architecture design; and (3) baselines ranging from tree-based methods to domain-generalization architectures. By bridging machine learning methodologies and Earth system science, CarbonBench aims to enable systematic comparison of transfer learning methods, serves as a testbed for regression under distribution shift, and contributes to the next-generation climate modeling efforts.

</details>


### [132] [Generative Drifting is Secretly Score Matching: a Spectral and Variational Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09936)
*Erkan Turan,Maks Ovsjanikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于核的漂移算子与平滑分布上的分数差异之间的等价关系，解决了原始工作中的三个关键理论问题，提出了指数带宽退火策略，并将漂移方法形式化为平滑KL散度的Wasserstein梯度流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成建模中的漂移方法虽然在一步图像生成中取得了最先进的效果，但其成功主要是经验性的，理论基础尚未得到充分理解。原始工作留下了三个关键问题：漂移消失是否保证分布相等、如何选择核函数、以及为什么停止梯度算子对稳定训练不可或缺。

Method: 通过观察高斯核下漂移算子等价于平滑分布上的分数差异，将漂移方法置于分数匹配框架中。通过线性化McKean-Vlasov动力学并在傅里叶空间中分析，揭示了频率相关的收敛时间尺度。提出了指数带宽退火策略σ(t)=σ₀e^{-rt}。将漂移形式化为平滑KL散度的Wasserstein梯度流，并基于JKO方案推导出停止梯度算子。

Result: 1) 证明了漂移消失确实保证分布相等；2) 揭示了高斯核存在指数级高频瓶颈，解释了经验上偏好拉普拉斯核的原因；3) 证明了停止梯度算子源于JKO方案要求的冻结场离散化，移除它会破坏梯度流保证；4) 提出的指数退火策略将收敛时间从exp(O(K_max²))减少到O(log K_max)；5) 提供了构建新漂移算子的通用模板，如Sinkhorn散度漂移。

Conclusion: 本文为漂移方法建立了坚实的理论基础，将其与分数匹配和Wasserstein梯度流联系起来，解决了原始工作的关键理论问题，提供了核选择的理论依据，并提出了改进收敛速度的实用策略，为构建新的漂移算子提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Generative Modeling via Drifting has recently achieved state-of-the-art one-step image generation through a kernel-based drift operator, yet the success is largely empirical and its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. In this paper, we make the following observation: \emph{under a Gaussian kernel, the drift operator is exactly a score difference on smoothed distributions}. This insight allows us to answer all three key questions left open in the original work: (1) whether a vanishing drift guarantees equality of distributions ($V_{p,q}=0\Rightarrow p=q$), (2) how to choose between kernels, and (3) why the stop-gradient operator is indispensable for stable training. Our observations position drifting within the well-studied score-matching family and enable a rich theoretical perspective. By linearizing the McKean-Vlasov dynamics and probing them in Fourier space, we reveal frequency-dependent convergence timescales comparable to \emph{Landau damping} in plasma kinetic theory: the Gaussian kernel suffers an exponential high-frequency bottleneck, explaining the empirical preference for the Laplacian kernel. We also propose an exponential bandwidth annealing schedule $σ(t)=σ_0 e^{-rt}$ that reduces convergence time from $\exp(O(K_{\max}^2))$ to $O(\log K_{\max})$. Finally, by formalizing drifting as a Wasserstein gradient flow of the smoothed KL divergence, we prove that the stop-gradient operator is derived directly from the frozen-field discretization mandated by the JKO scheme, and removing it severs training from any gradient-flow guarantee. This variational perspective further provides a general template for constructing novel drift operators, demonstrated with a Sinkhorn divergence drift.

</details>


### [133] [SignalMC-MED: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Biosignal Foundation Models on Single-Lead ECG and PPG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09940)
*Fredrik K. Gustafsson,Xiao Gu,Mattia Carletti,Patitapaban Palo,David W. Eyre,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SignalMC-MED是一个用于评估生物信号基础模型在同步单导联心电图和光电容积脉搏波数据上的基准测试，包含22,256次就诊记录和20个临床相关任务，评估显示领域特定模型优于通用时间序列模型，多模态融合优于单模态输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生物信号基础模型在各种临床预测任务中表现出良好性能，但在长时程多模态数据上的系统评估仍然有限。需要建立一个标准化基准来评估这些模型在真实临床环境中的表现。

Method: 基于MC-MED数据集构建SignalMC-MED基准，包含22,256次就诊的10分钟重叠ECG和PPG信号，涵盖20个临床相关任务。系统评估代表性时间序列和生物信号基础模型在ECG-only、PPG-only和ECG+PPG三种设置下的表现。

Result: 领域特定生物信号基础模型始终优于通用时间序列模型；多模态ECG+PPG融合比单模态输入有稳健改进；使用完整10分钟信号优于较短片段；较大模型变体并不总是优于较小变体；手工制作的ECG领域特征提供强基线，与学习到的FM表示结合具有互补价值。

Conclusion: SignalMC-MED为生物信号基础模型评估提供了标准化基准，并为评估和部署这些模型提供了实用指导，强调领域特定模型、多模态融合和完整信号长度的重要性。

Abstract: Recent biosignal foundation models (FMs) have demonstrated promising performance across diverse clinical prediction tasks, yet systematic evaluation on long-duration multimodal data remains limited. We introduce SignalMC-MED, a benchmark for evaluating biosignal FMs on synchronized single-lead electrocardiogram (ECG) and photoplethysmogram (PPG) data. Derived from the MC-MED dataset, SignalMC-MED comprises 22,256 visits with 10-minute overlapping ECG and PPG signals, and includes 20 clinically relevant tasks spanning prediction of demographics, emergency department disposition, laboratory value regression, and detection of prior ICD-10 diagnoses. Using this benchmark, we perform a systematic evaluation of representative time-series and biosignal FMs across ECG-only, PPG-only, and ECG + PPG settings. We find that domain-specific biosignal FMs consistently outperform general time-series models, and that multimodal ECG + PPG fusion yields robust improvements over unimodal inputs. Moreover, using the full 10-minute signal consistently outperforms shorter segments, and larger model variants do not reliably outperform smaller ones. Hand-crafted ECG domain features provide a strong baseline and offer complementary value when combined with learned FM representations. Together, these results establish SignalMC-MED as a standardized benchmark and provide practical guidance for evaluating and deploying biosignal FMs.

</details>


### [134] [Towards a Neural Debugger for Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09951)
*Maximilian Beck,Jonas Gehring,Jannik Kossen,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出神经调试器，这是一种能够模拟传统调试器操作的语言模型，支持逐步执行、设置断点等功能，既能预测程序未来状态也能推断先前状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经解释器方法缺乏交互控制能力，无法像开发者使用调试器那样在特定断点停止执行并逐步检查相关代码段。需要开发能够模拟传统调试器操作的神经模型。

Method: 通过微调大型语言模型或从头预训练较小模型来创建神经调试器，支持步入、步过、步出函数以及在特定源代码行设置断点等调试操作。

Result: 在CruxEval基准测试中，神经调试器在输出预测和输入预测任务上均表现出色，展示了强大的条件执行建模能力。

Conclusion: 神经调试器为未来智能编码系统奠定了基础，可作为模拟调试环境的世界模型，为代码生成、程序理解和自动化调试提供支持。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) on Python execution traces grounds them in code execution and enables the line-by-line execution prediction of whole Python programs, effectively turning them into neural interpreters (FAIR CodeGen Team et al., 2025). However, developers rarely execute programs step by step; instead, they use debuggers to stop execution at certain breakpoints and step through relevant portions only while inspecting or modifying program variables. Existing neural interpreter approaches lack such interactive control. To address this limitation, we introduce neural debuggers: language models that emulate traditional debuggers, supporting operations such as stepping into, over, or out of functions, as well as setting breakpoints at specific source lines. We show that neural debuggers -- obtained via fine-tuning large LLMs or pre-training smaller models from scratch -- can reliably model both forward execution (predicting future states and outputs) and inverse execution (inferring prior states or inputs) conditioned on debugger actions. Evaluated on CruxEval, our models achieve strong performance on both output and input prediction tasks, demonstrating robust conditional execution modeling. Our work takes first steps towards future agentic coding systems in which neural debuggers serve as a world model for simulated debugging environments, providing execution feedback or enabling agents to interact with real debugging tools. This capability lays the foundation for more powerful code generation, program understanding, and automated debugging.

</details>


### [135] [From Data Statistics to Feature Geometry: How Correlations Shape Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09972)
*Lucas Prieto,Edward Stevinson,Melih Barsbey,Tolga Birdal,Pedro A. M. Mediano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文挑战了传统机制可解释性中关于特征叠加的假设，提出在相关特征存在时，特征间的干扰可以是建设性的而非噪声，并通过BOWS实验展示了这种结构在真实数据中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机制可解释性研究特征叠加时假设特征是稀疏且不相关的，这种理想化设置无法解释真实语言模型中观察到的语义聚类和循环结构。作者旨在探索相关特征存在时特征叠加的真实行为。

Method: 提出了Bag-of-Words Superposition (BOWS)这一受控设置，用于在叠加中编码互联网文本的二进制词袋表示。通过该设置研究相关特征下的特征几何排列，分析权重衰减训练对特征结构的影响。

Result: 发现当特征相关时，特征间的干扰可以是建设性的而非噪声。特征根据其共激活模式排列，使得活跃特征间的干扰具有建设性，同时仍使用ReLU避免假阳性。这种排列在权重衰减训练的模型中更普遍，自然产生语义聚类和循环结构。

Conclusion: 传统特征叠加理论对真实数据的解释不完整。相关特征下的建设性干扰机制能够解释真实语言模型中观察到的语义聚类和循环结构，为机制可解释性提供了更符合实际情况的理论框架。

Abstract: A central idea in mechanistic interpretability is that neural networks represent more features than they have dimensions, arranging them in superposition to form an over-complete basis. This framing has been influential, motivating dictionary learning approaches such as sparse autoencoders. However, superposition has mostly been studied in idealized settings where features are sparse and uncorrelated. In these settings, superposition is typically understood as introducing interference that must be minimized geometrically and filtered out by non-linearities such as ReLUs, yielding local structures like regular polytopes. We show that this account is incomplete for realistic data by introducing Bag-of-Words Superposition (BOWS), a controlled setting to encode binary bag-of-words representations of internet text in superposition. Using BOWS, we find that when features are correlated, interference can be constructive rather than just noise to be filtered out. This is achieved by arranging features according to their co-activation patterns, making interference between active features constructive, while still using ReLUs to avoid false positives. We show that this kind of arrangement is more prevalent in models trained with weight decay and naturally gives rise to semantic clusters and cyclical structures which have been observed in real language models yet were not explained by the standard picture of superposition. Code for this paper can be found at https://github.com/LucasPrietoAl/correlations-feature-geometry.

</details>


### [136] [Task Aware Modulation Using Representation Learning for Upsaling of Terrestrial Carbon Fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09974)
*Aleksei Rozanov,Arvind Renganathan,Vipin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TAM-RL框架通过结合时空表征学习和物理约束，显著提升了全球碳通量估算的准确性和可迁移性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确估算全球碳通量对全球碳预算至关重要，但现有数据驱动方法由于地面测量数据稀疏且分布不均，难以泛化到未观测区域，存在系统性区域偏差和高预测不确定性

Method: 提出任务感知调制与表征学习框架，将时空表征学习与基于碳平衡方程的知识引导编码器-解码器架构和损失函数相结合

Result: 在150多个代表不同生物群落和气候区的通量塔站点上，TAM-RL相比现有最先进数据集，RMSE降低8-9.6%，解释方差从19.4%提升至43.8%

Conclusion: 将物理基础约束与自适应表征学习相结合，能够显著增强全球碳通量估算的鲁棒性和可迁移性

Abstract: Accurately upscaling terrestrial carbon fluxes is central to estimating the global carbon budget, yet remains challenging due to the sparse and regionally biased distribution of ground measurements. Existing data-driven upscaling products often fail to generalize beyond observed domains, leading to systematic regional biases and high predictive uncertainty. We introduce Task-Aware Modulation with Representation Learning (TAM-RL), a framework that couples spatio-temporal representation learning with knowledge-guided encoder-decoder architecture and loss function derived from the carbon balance equation. Across 150+ flux tower sites representing diverse biomes and climate regimes, TAM-RL improves predictive performance relative to existing state-of-the-art datasets, reducing RMSE by 8-9.6% and increasing explained variance ($R^2$) from 19.4% to 43.8%, depending on the target flux. These results demonstrate that integrating physically grounded constraints with adaptive representation learning can substantially enhance the robustness and transferability of global carbon flux estimates.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Data-driven robust Markov decision processes on Borel spaces: performance guarantees via an axiomatic approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08979)
*Sivaramakrishnan Ramani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 数据驱动的鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程：通过经验分布构建模糊集，证明鲁棒最优值函数收敛于真实最优值，并提供有限样本性能保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有未知扰动分布的马尔可夫决策过程问题，传统经验MDP方法在有限样本下无法提供性能保证，需要鲁棒MDP方法来处理分布不确定性

Method: 构建未知扰动分布的经验分布，以经验分布为中心的非负距离函数子水平集作为模糊集，连接分布的弱收敛与距离函数收敛，分析满足特定集中不等式的距离函数

Result: 证明鲁棒最优值函数和样本外值函数随样本量增加收敛于真实最优值函数；有限样本下鲁棒最优值函数以高概率作为样本外值函数上界；获得概率收敛率、样本复杂度界限和分布外性能界限

Conclusion: 数据驱动的鲁棒MDP方法优于经验MDP，能提供有限样本性能保证，多种文献中研究的距离函数满足要求，为处理未知分布MDP问题提供了理论保证

Abstract: We consider Markov decision processes (MDPs) with unknown disturbance distribution and address this problem using the robust Markov decision process (RMDP) approach. We construct the empirical distribution of the unknown disturbance distribution and characterize our ambiguity set of distributions as the sublevel set of a nonnegative distance function from the empirical distribution. By connecting the weak convergence of distributions to convergence with respect to the distance function, we prove that the robust optimal value function and the out-of-sample value function converge to the true optimal value function with increasing sample-sizes. We establish that, for finite sample-sizes, the robust optimal value function serves as a high probability upper bound on the out-of-sample value function. We also obtain probabilistic convergence rates, sample complexity bounds, and out-of-distribution performance bounds. The finite sample performance guarantees rely on the distance function satisfying a certain concentration type inequality. Several well-studied distances in the literature meet the requirements imposed on the distance function. We also analyze the data-driven properties of empirical MDPs and demonstrate that, unlike our data-driven RMDPs, empirical MDPs fail to satisfy some of the finite sample performance guarantees.

</details>


### [138] [On the solvability of parameter estimation-based observers for nonlinear systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09076)
*Bowen Yi,Leyan Fang,Romeo Ortega*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文深入分析了参数估计观测器(PEBO)在非线性系统中的存在性条件，重点研究了变换性和可辨识性这两个关键性质，并给出了确保PEBO存在的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PEBO是一种新兴的非线性系统状态观测器设计工具，它将状态估计问题转化为在线参数辨识问题，有效解决了实际应用中的许多估计难题。然而，PEBO设计的可行性依赖于两个基本性质：变换性和可辨识性。本文旨在深入分析这两个性质，为一般非线性系统提供PEBO存在的充分条件。

Method: 通过详细研究变换性和可辨识性这两个关键性质来分析方法。变换性涉及寻找合适偏微分方程的注入解的存在性，而可辨识性则关注由此产生的非线性回归模型参数化的唯一性。研究为这两个性质提供了充分的数学条件。

Result: 分析表明，PEBO的存在性取决于变换性和可辨识性这两个性质的满足。研究为一般非线性系统提供了确保这些性质成立的充分条件，从而为PEBO的设计和应用奠定了理论基础。

Conclusion: 本文系统分析了PEBO在非线性系统中的存在性问题，明确了变换性和可辨识性这两个关键性质的重要性，并给出了确保PEBO存在的充分条件，为非线性系统状态观测器的设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Parameter estimation-based observer (PEBO) is a recently developed constructive tool to design state observers for nonlinear systems. It reformulates the state estimation problem as one of online parameter identification, effectively addressing many open estimation challenges in practical applications. The feasibility of a PEBO design relies on two fundamental properties: transformability and identifiability. The former pertains to the existence of an injective solution to a suitable partial differential equation, whereas the latter characterizes the uniqueness of the parameterization induced by the resulting nonlinear regression model. In this paper, we analyze the existence of PEBOs for general nonlinear systems by studying these two properties in detail and by providing sufficient conditions under which they hold.

</details>


### [139] [Telehealth Control Policies: Bridging the Gap Between Patients and Doctors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09299)
*Shuwen Lu,Mark E. Lewis,Jamol Pender*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究两阶段排队系统中护士与全科医生协作决策问题，开发线性时间可计算的启发式策略，在参数变化时保持稳健，性能接近最优（平均误差0.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVS MinuteClinics等医疗环境中，护士需要决定是否独立治疗低危患者或通过远程医疗与全科医生协作。协作虽能提高服务质量但可能引入等待延迟，需要在服务质量与系统效率间权衡。现有策略对参数变化敏感，需要开发稳健的决策方案。

Method: 分析两阶段排队系统的最优策略结构特性，特别关注上游队列较大的情况。基于结构洞察设计线性时间可计算的启发式策略，适用于实际部署。通过理论分析和数值实验验证策略性能。

Result: 启发式策略在整个参数空间内表现稳健，平均性能仅比最优策略低0.1%。常用基准策略对参数变化高度敏感，成本可能比最优策略高出100%以上。研究识别了远程医疗基础设施投资的关键场景。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了实用的决策指导，帮助护士在参数变化时做出稳健决策。同时为利益相关者提供了远程医疗基础设施投资的价值评估框架，识别了投资必要与不必要的具体场景。

Abstract: This paper studies a sequential decision-making problem in a two-stage queueing system modeled after operations in CVS MinuteClinics, where nurse practitioners (NPs) oversee patient care throughout the entire visit. All services are non-preemptive, and NPs cannot begin treating a new patient until the current patient has completed both stages of care. Following an initial diagnosis in the upstream phase, NPs must decide for low-acuity patients whether to proceed with treatment independently through immediate service, or to collaborate with a dedicated general physician (GP) via telemedicine. While collaboration typically improves service quality and is preferred by individual patients, it may introduce delays as the NP-patient pair waits for a GP to become available. This work explores the structural properties of optimal policies under different system parameters, with a focus on large initial upstream queues, revealing unconventional and complex policy behaviors. Leveraging these structural insights and supporting theoretical results, we design simple and effective heuristics that are computable in linear time and suitable for practical implementation. These heuristics are robust across the entire parameter space of interest, and offer clear, actionable guidance for NPs as system parameters vary. They also achieve near-optimal performance, averaging within 0.1% of the optimal, while commonly used benchmark policies are highly sensitive to parameter shifts and can incur costs more than 100% higher than optimal. The work provides applicable insights for decision-makers on improving policy robustness and effectiveness, as well as recommendations for stakeholders on the value of investing in telemedicine infrastructure. For instance, we identify scenarios where such investments may be either unnecessary or essential based on specific system parameters.

</details>


### [140] [On the Closed-Form Solution for Robust Adaptive Beamforming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09300)
*Licheng Zhao,Rui Zhou,Wenqiang Pu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种用于鲁棒自适应波束形成问题的三阶段闭式解方案，相比现有方法更高效且能处理秩亏协方差矩阵


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒自适应波束形成问题的解决方案存在局限性：MOSEK使用通用内点法效率不高，RMVB算法仅适用于满秩协方差矩阵场景，需要更高效且通用的解决方案

Method: 提出包含三个连续阶段的新闭式解方案：对角化变换、相位对齐和KKT求解，专门针对RAB问题设计，推导过程比RMVB更简单

Result: 新方案在计算时间上优于现有基准方法，同时保持最优性，能够处理秩亏协方差矩阵场景，并首次揭示了存在性和唯一性条件

Conclusion: 提出的三阶段闭式解方案为鲁棒自适应波束形成问题提供了更高效、更通用的解决方案，填补了现有方法的局限性

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the classical robust adaptive beamforming (RAB) problem. Conventionally, this problem is solved either with an off-the-shelf solver like MOSEK or through the well-known RMVB algorithm based on Lagrange multiplier approaches. The solver MOSEK is implemented with the general interior point method and RMVB is only limited to the full-rank covariance scenario. In order to improve the existing benchmarks, we develop a novel closed-form solution scheme containing three consecutive stages: Diagonalization Transform, Phase Alignment, and KKT Solution. The proposed scheme is specifically intended for the RAB problem and thus more efficient than MOSEK. Moreover, the derivation process is simpler than RMVB and the output solution can cover the rank-deficient covariance scenario in extra. Aside from a new solution, we manage to unveil the existence and uniqueness conditions, which have never been studied before. The simulation results show that the proposed solution improves the existing benchmarks in terms of computational time while maintaining optimality.

</details>


### [141] [On Real-Time Feasibility of High-Rate MPC using an Active-Set Method on Nano-Quadcopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09342)
*Joel Wikner,Daniel Arnström,Daniel Axehill*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 在Crazyflie 2.1纳米四旋翼上成功部署双主动集求解器(DAQP)实现500Hz高频控制，相比ADMM方法执行时间更短，并引入基于PCA的数据驱动集选择方法实现实时可行性认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在纳米级飞行平台上部署模型预测控制面临严重计算限制的挑战，现有方法要么使用一阶方法进行低级稳定，要么将主动集求解器限制在高级控制中，需要探索更高效的求解方法。

Method: 1. 在STM32F405微控制器上部署双主动集求解器(DAQP)实现500Hz高频控制；2. 与最先进的ADMM求解器(TinyMPC)进行直接基准测试；3. 引入基于主成分分析的数据驱动集选择方法，用于管理求解器实时可行性认证的复杂性。

Result: 1. 双主动集方法在考虑飞行包线下执行时间低于ADMM方法；2. 数据驱动集选择方法使最先进的实时认证方法能够在飞行前离线确认或拒绝实时可行性；3. 实验验证了主动集方法在资源受限硬件上进行高频控制的竞争力。

Conclusion: 主动集方法是资源受限硬件上高频控制的高度竞争性替代方案，其实时认证可能性增加了可靠性，为纳米级飞行平台的模型预测控制部署提供了新途径。

Abstract: Deploying Model Predictive Control on nano-scale aerial platforms is challenging due to the severe computational limitations of onboard microcontrollers. This paper presents an experimental study with computational focus of a dual active-set solver (DAQP) applied to the low-level control of a Crazyflie 2.1 nano-quadcopter. Unlike previous approaches that utilize first-order methods for low-level stabilization or restrict active-set solvers to high-level control, this work demonstrates the successful deployment of a dual active-set method executing at 500 Hz on an STM32F405 microcontroller. We provide a direct benchmark against a state-of-the-art ADMM-based solver (TinyMPC), revealing that the active-set method yields lower execution times for the flight envelopes considered. Furthermore, to manage the complexity of certifying the solver's real-time feasibility, we introduce a data-driven set selection method using Principal Component Analysis. Using this approach, state-of-the-art real-time certification methods can be applied to confirm or reject real-time feasibility already off-line prior to flight. More generally, the experimental results validate that active-set methods are a highly competitive alternative for high-frequency control on resource-constrained hardware and the possibility for their real-time certification adds reliability.

</details>


### [142] [SHANG++: Robust Stochastic Acceleration under Multiplicative Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09355)
*Yaxin Yu,Long Chen,Minfu Feng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出SHANG和SHANG++两种加速随机梯度下降方法，通过离散化Hessian驱动的Nesterov加速梯度流，在乘性噪声条件下实现更好的稳定性和收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在乘性噪声缩放条件下，原始的Nesterov加速方法对噪声敏感，当梯度噪声超过信号时可能发散。需要开发更鲁棒的加速随机优化方法。

Method: 1. 离散化Hessian驱动的Nesterov加速梯度流；2. 推导SHANG方法（Gauss-Seidel型离散化）；3. 提出SHANG++方法（增加阻尼校正）；4. 建立凸和强凸目标在MNS下的收敛保证。

Result: SHANG++在凸问题和深度学习应用中表现一致良好；在ResNet-34的噪声实验中，单一超参数配置达到与无噪声设置1%以内的准确率；在所有实验中，SHANG++在鲁棒性和效率上优于现有加速方法，参数敏感性最小。

Conclusion: 通过离散化Hessian驱动的Nesterov加速梯度流并引入阻尼校正，SHANG++实现了在乘性噪声条件下的快速收敛和强噪声鲁棒性，为加速随机优化提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Under the multiplicative noise scaling (MNS) condition, original Nesterov acceleration is provably sensitive to noise and may diverge when gradient noise overwhelms the signal. In this paper, we develop two accelerated stochastic gradient descent methods by discretizing the Hessian-driven Nesterov accelerated gradient flow. We first derive SHANG, a direct Gauss-Seidel-type discretization that already improves stability under MNS. We then introduce SHANG++, which adds a damping correction and achieves faster convergence with stronger noise robustness. We establish convergence guarantees for both convex and strongly convex objectives under MNS, together with explicit parameter choices. In our experiments, SHANG++ performs consistently well across convex problems and applications in deep learning. In a dedicated noise experiment on ResNet-34, a single hyperparameter configuration attains accuracy within 1% of the noise-free setting. Across all experiments, SHANG++ outperforms existing accelerated methods in robustness and efficiency, with minimal parameter sensitivity.

</details>


### [143] [Convex Duality Made Difficult](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09429)
*Eigil Fjeldgren Rischel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出用范畴论方法研究凸优化问题，构建以优化问题为对象的范畴，并用范畴论工具重新推导已有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸优化是应用数学的重要领域，但应用范畴论学者对此研究不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过范畴论方法研究优化问题，提供新的理论框架。

Method: 构建以优化问题为对象的范畴，使用范畴论工具分析优化问题。通过范畴论方法重新推导已有结果，包括极小极大定理和勒让德对偶的性质。

Result: 成功构建了优化问题的范畴框架，并用范畴论方法重新推导了极小极大定理（定理5.5）以及凸函数的勒让德对偶性质（f**=f，命题6.6）。

Conclusion: 范畴论为凸优化研究提供了新的理论工具和视角，能够统一推导已有结果，展示了范畴论在优化理论中的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: The study of convex functions - in particular, of their optimization (really minimization) is one of the most important fields of applied mathematics. Convexity seems to be one of those incredibly well-chosen hypotheses which is just specific enough to admit a wealth of theorems, just general enough to produce a nontrivial theory (and a large amount of important examples).
  Convex optimization, possibly because it has an "analytical" rather than "algebraic" feel, has not been very thoroughly studied by applied category theorists. The one notable exception is [4], which studies the decomposition of optimization problems by categorical means. This paper takes a different approach, attempting to define a category with optimization problems as the objects, and to prove theorems about optimization by categorical means. As an illustration, we show how to use our methods to rederive some existing results: A minimax-type theorem, Theorem 5.5, and the fact that for convex functions, (f*)*=f (where f* is the Legendre dual), Proposition 6.6.

</details>


### [144] [Faster Stochastic ADMM for Nonsmooth Composite Convex Optimization in Hilbert Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09447)
*Weihua Deng,Haiming Song,Hao Wang,Jinda Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于希尔伯特空间中非光滑复合随机凸优化的随机交替方向乘子法，证明了强凸情况下的强收敛性，并展示了更快的非遍历收敛速率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受具有随机系数的偏微分方程约束优化问题驱动，需要解决希尔伯特空间中的非光滑复合随机凸优化问题

Method: 提出随机交替方向乘子法，适用于希尔伯特空间中的非光滑复合随机凸优化问题，证明了强凸情况下的强收敛性，并展示了非遍历收敛速率

Result: 证明了强凸情况下的强收敛性，展示了在函数值和可行性违反方面更快的非遍历收敛速率，提供了模型问题的应用和大偏差概率界限，初步数值结果显示了方法的效率

Conclusion: 提出的随机ADMM方法能有效解决具有随机系数的PDE约束优化问题，在强凸和一般凸情况下都具有良好的收敛性能

Abstract: In this paper, a stochastic alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) is proposed for a class of nonsmooth composite and stochastic convex optimization problems in Hilbert space, motivated by optimization problems constrained by partial differential equation (PDE) with random coefficients. We prove the strong convergence of the proposed ADMM algorithm in the strongly convex case, and show the faster nonergodic convergence rates in terms of functional values and feasibility violation for both strongly convex and general convex cases. We demonstrate the application of the proposed method to solve certain model problems, along with its associated probability bound of large deviation. Some preliminary numerical results illustrate the efficiency of our method.

</details>


### [145] [Mollified Christoffel-Darboux Kernels and Density Recovery on Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09462)
*Leandro Bentancur,Didier Henrion,Mauricio Velasco*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文引入了代数簇上的磨光Christoffel-Darboux核，通过系统正则化改进经典CD核，实现支撑内外更清晰的分界和密度恢复的定量控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主要动机有两个：一是改进经典CD多项式在支撑内外的分界性质，将支撑内的线性增长替换为一致有界；二是在不知道底层域平衡测度的情况下，从矩数据中获得一致且定量可控的密度恢复。

Method: 1. 在代数簇上引入磨光函数族；2. 对每个测度和度数定义磨光CD核，可通过线性代数从矩数据计算；3. 在单位球面上使用由带状多项式构造的代数磨光器获得改进的收敛速率。

Result: 1. 证明了改进的分界性质：在支撑内部磨光CD多项式在度数上一致有界，在支撑外部指数增长；2. 在Sobolev正则性假设下，推导了欧氏空间中密度恢复的显式收敛速率；3. 在单位球面上，特定磨光器可获得改进的收敛速率。

Conclusion: 磨光CD核提供了一种系统正则化方法，改进了经典CD核的分界性质，实现了无需平衡测度知识的定量密度恢复，在单位球面上可获得最优收敛速率。

Abstract: We introduce mollified Christoffel-Darboux (CD) kernels on varieties, a systematic regularization of the classical CD kernel associated with a probability measure on a compact domain. The main motivations are twofold: first, to sharpen the classical on/off-support dichotomy of the CD polynomial by replacing linear growth on the support by a uniform bound; second, to obtain consistent and quantitatively controlled recovery of densities from moment data, without the need to know the equilibrium measure of the underlying domain. Our contributions are the following: (i) We introduce families of mollifiers on algebraic varieties. For each measure and degree on such a variety we define a mollified CD kernel, which can be computed from the moments of the underlying measure by linear algebra. (ii) We prove, by elementary arguments, that an improved dichotomy property holds: on the interior of the support the mollified CD polynomial is uniformly bounded in the degree, while outside the support it grows exponentially with the degree. (iii) Assuming Sobolev regularity of the density with respect to a reference measure, we derive explicit convergence rates for density recovery for measures in Euclidean space via mollified CD kernel. (iv) On the unit sphere, we show that suitably chosen algebraic mollifiers, constructed from zonal polynomials, lead to kernels with improved rates, building on classical constructive approximation results.

</details>


### [146] [Fairness in Robust Unit Commitment Problem Considering Suppression of Renewable Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09464)
*Ichiro Toyoshima,Pierre-Louis Poirion,Tomohide Yamazaki,Kota Yaguchi,Masayuki Kubota,Ryota Mizutani,Akiko Takeda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出RE-RPfair模型，在原有可再生能源鲁棒优化问题基础上加入公平性约束，使用基尼指数衡量光伏分配公平度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机组组合问题面临负荷不确定性和可再生能源扰动，原有RE-RP模型解决了不确定性抑制问题，但缺乏对光伏分配公平性的考虑

Method: 提出RE-RPfair模型，作为RE-RP的扩展，引入公平性约束，使用基尼指数衡量光伏分配公平程度

Result: 通过仿真验证了RE-RPfair模型的有效性，实现了光伏分配的公平性

Conclusion: RE-RPfair模型在解决机组组合不确定性的同时，实现了光伏分配的公平性，基尼指数是有效的公平性衡量指标

Abstract: Power company operators make power generation plans one day in advance, in what is known as the Unit Commitment (UC) problem. UC is exposed to uncertainties, such as unknown electricity load and disturbances caused by renewable energy sources, especially PVs. In previous research, we proposed the Renewable Energy Robust Optimization Problem (RE-RP), which solves these uncertainties by considering suppression. In this paper, we propose a new model called RE-RP with fairness (RE-RPfair), which aims to achieve fair allocation among PVs allocation. This model is an expansion of the original RE-RP, and we prove its effectiveness through simulation. To measure the degree of fairness, we use the Gini Index, which is well-known in social science.

</details>


### [147] [An Integer Linear Programming Model for the Evolomino Puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09483)
*Andrei V. Nikolaev,Yuri A. Myasnikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将Evolomino谜题形式化为整数线性规划模型，并开发随机实例生成算法，通过计算实验验证CP-SAT求解器能高效求解该谜题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Evolomino是Nikoli公司推出的纸笔逻辑谜题，具有文化独立性。研究旨在为该谜题建立形式化数学模型，并开发可确保解唯一性的实例生成方法。

Method: 1. 将Evolomino规则形式化为整数线性规划模型，通过线性约束编码块演化、连通性和一致性要求；2. 基于ILP框架设计随机实例生成算法，确保解的唯一性；3. 在自定义基准数据集上进行计算实验。

Result: 计算实验表明，先进的CP-SAT求解器能在1秒内处理11×11网格的谜题实例，在1分钟内处理18×18网格的实例。

Conclusion: 成功将Evolomino谜题形式化为ILP模型，并开发了有效的实例生成算法。CP-SAT求解器能高效求解该谜题，验证了所提方法的实用性。

Abstract: Evolomino is a pencil-and-paper logic puzzle published by the Japanese company Nikoli, renowned for culture-independent puzzles such as Sudoku, Kakuro, and Slitherlink. Its name reflects the core mechanic: the polyomino-like blocks drawn by the player must gradually "evolve" according to the directions indicated by arrows pre-printed on a rectangular grid.
  In this paper, we formalize the rules of Evolomino as an integer linear programming (ILP) model, encoding block evolution, connectivity, and consistency requirements through linear constraints. Furthermore, we introduce an algorithm for generating random Evolomino instances, utilizing this ILP framework to ensure solution uniqueness. Computational experiments on a custom benchmark dataset demonstrate that a state-of-the-art CP-SAT solver successfully handles puzzle instances of up to $11 \times 11$ within one second and up to $18 \times 18$ within one minute.

</details>


### [148] [Dirichlet control problems with energy regularization governed by non-coercive elliptic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09507)
*Thomas Apel,Mariano Mateos,Arnd Rösch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究非凸多边形域上非强制性椭圆方程控制的线性二次Dirichlet控制问题，使用能量半范数进行Tikhonov正则化，通过加权Sobolev空间分析解的正则性，采用分级网格恢复最优收敛率，引入离散投影处理非齐次边界条件，获得最优误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非凸多边形域上非强制性椭圆方程控制的Dirichlet控制问题，这类问题在非凸域中面临正则性降低和收敛率下降的挑战，需要开发能恢复最优收敛率的数值方法。

Method: 采用能量半范数进行Tikhonov正则化；在加权Sobolev空间中建立解的正则性；使用有限元离散化；在非凸多边形域中采用分级网格恢复最优收敛率；引入H^{1/2}(Γ)意义下的离散投影处理非齐次边界条件。

Result: 建立了离散问题关于离散化参数的一致强凸性；获得了最优误差估计；验证了分级网格在非凸域中恢复最优收敛率的有效性；讨论了实际计算考虑和数值示例。

Conclusion: 通过结合加权Sobolev空间理论、分级网格技术和离散投影方法，成功解决了非凸多边形域上非强制性椭圆方程Dirichlet控制问题的数值分析，获得了最优收敛率，为这类问题的数值求解提供了有效框架。

Abstract: The present study investigates a linear-quadratic Dirichlet control problem governed by a non-coercive elliptic equation posed on a possibly non-convex polygonal domain. Tikhonov regularization is carried out in an energy seminorm. The regularity of the solutions is established in appropriate weighted Sobolev spaces, and the finite element discretization of the problem is analyzed.
  In order to recover the optimal rate of convergence in polygonal non-convex domains, graded meshes are required. In addressing this particular problem, it is also necessary to introduce a discrete projection in the sense of $H^{1/2}(Γ)$ to deal with the non-homogeneous boundary condition.
  A thorough examination of the approximation properties of the discrete controls reveals that the discrete problems are strongly convex uniformly with respect to the discretization parameter.
  All these ingredients lead to optimal error estimates. Practical computational considerations and numerical examples are discussed at the end of the paper.

</details>


### [149] [A Unifying Primal-Dual Proximal Framework for Distributed Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09524)
*Zichong Ou,Jie Lu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出统一原始-对偶近端(UPP)框架，处理分布式非凸优化问题，统一多种一阶和二阶方法，并开发了UPP-MC和UPP-SC两种变体，证明其收敛性，进一步优化得到通信复杂度最优的UPP-SC-OPT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决分布式非凸优化问题，其中每个节点拥有私有局部目标函数，仅能与邻居节点通信，需要集体找到共同最优解。现有方法缺乏统一框架，且通信效率有待提升。

Method: 通过线性化增广拉格朗日函数并引入时变近端项，构建统一原始-对偶近端(UPP)框架。基于该框架开发两种具体实现：UPP-MC（多通信）和UPP-SC（单通信）。进一步将切比雪夫加速技术融入UPP-SC，得到UPP-SC-OPT以优化混合过程。

Result: 证明UPP-MC和UPP-SC在非凸光滑问题上以次线性速率达到平稳解。在Polyak-Łojasiewics条件下，UPP-MC线性收敛到全局最优。UPP-SC-OPT获得最优通信复杂度边界。实验表明所提算法在收敛速度和通信效率上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出的UPP框架统一了多种分布式优化方法，其具体实现UPP-MC和UPP-SC在非凸问题中具有理论保证的收敛性，而UPP-SC-OPT进一步优化了通信效率，为分布式非凸优化提供了高效且理论完备的解决方案。

Abstract: We consider distributed nonconvex optimization over an undirected network, where each node privately possesses its local objective and communicates exclusively with its neighboring nodes, striving to collectively achieve a common optimal solution. To handle the nonconvexity of the objective, we linearize the augmented Lagrangian function and introduce a time-varying proximal term. This approach leads to a Unifying Primal-Dual Proximal (UPP) framework that unifies a variety of existing first-order and second-order methods. Building on this framework, we further derive two specialized realizations with different communication strategies, namely UPP-MC and UPP-SC. We prove that both UPP-MC and UPP-SC achieve stationary solutions for nonconvex smooth problems at a sublinear rate. Furthermore, under the additional Polyak-Łojasiewics (P-Ł) condition, UPP-MC is linearly convergent to the global optimum. These convergence results provide new or improved guarantees for many existing methods that can be viewed as specializations of UPP-MC or UPP-SC. To further optimize the mixing process, we incorporate Chebyshev acceleration into UPP-SC, resulting in UPP-SC-OPT, which attains an optimal communication complexity bound. Extensive experiments across diverse network topologies demonstrate that our proposed algorithms outperform state-of-the-art methods in both convergence speed and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [150] [One-Way Thermo-Mechanical Coupled System Identification Using Displacement and Temperature Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09526)
*Talhah Shamshad Ali Ansari,Suneth Warnakulasuriya,Ihar Antonau,Harbir Antil,Rainald Löhner,Roland Wüchner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于伴随方法的优化驱动高保真系统识别框架，用于在热载荷存在下同时定位结构弱点和恢复温度场


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热载荷下的结构系统识别具有挑战性，未测量或建模不良的热效应会掩盖或模拟损伤，导致不可靠的结论。需要开发能够同时处理热-机械耦合效应的识别方法

Method: 提出两种策略：1) 整体方法，同时识别杨氏模量和温度分布；2) 分区方法，通过Gauss-Seidel型定点方案迭代耦合两个不精确子问题。基于优化框架最小化模拟响应与稀疏位移和温度传感器测量数据之间的加权差异

Result: 在Plate With a Hole和Footbridge两个数值算例中，两种方法均成功恢复了杨氏模量和温度分布，即使传感器布置未能完全捕捉潜在热趋势。相比恒定温度假设和传感器数据插值，该方法实现了最准确的损伤定位和温度重建

Conclusion: 提出的优化驱动伴随方法能够有效处理热-机械耦合结构识别问题。温度传感器位置与数量同等重要：良好布置的传感器显著改善识别效果，而错过关键热特征的额外传感器提供有限益处。在局部热特征传感器采样不足时，该方法相比传统方法优势最大

Abstract: Structural system identification in the presence of thermal loads is challenging, as unmeasured or poorly modeled thermal effects can mask or mimic damage, leading to unreliable conclusions. This work presents an optimization-driven, adjoint-based high-fidelity system identification framework for localizing structural weakness and recovering the temperature field in one-way thermo-mechanical coupled structures. The methodology builds upon a standard optimization formulation that minimizes weighted discrepancies between simulated responses and measured data from a sparse displacement and temperature sensor network. To account for thermal effects, two strategies are proposed: a monolithic approach, which simultaneously identifies Young's modulus and temperature distributions, and a partitioned approach, which iteratively couples two inexact sub-problems through a Gauss-Seidel type fixed-point scheme. The proposed approaches are evaluated using two numerical examples -- a Plate With a Hole and a Footbridge model -- under linearly varying and localized thermal fields, and for different sensor layouts. Both approaches successfully recover the Young's modulus and temperature distributions, even when sensor placement does not fully capture the underlying thermal trends. Compared with a constant-temperature assumption and interpolation of the temperature field from sensor data, the proposed approach achieves the most accurate damage localization and temperature reconstruction. The largest gains occur when localized thermal features are poorly sampled by sensors, where interpolation and constant-temperature assumptions underperform. Furthermore, results show that the location of the temperature sensors is as influential as the number of sensors: well-placed sensors substantially improve identification, while additional sensors that miss critical thermal features provide limited benefit.

</details>


### [151] [Refined Estimates on the Dimensions of Maximal Faces of Completely Positive Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09633)
*O. I. Kostyukova,T. V. Tchemisova*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了完全正矩阵锥的最大面的维度，为奇数维证明了精确下界为n，为偶数维(n≥8)给出了介于n和n+3之间的新上界估计，显著改进了现有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全正矩阵锥的最大面结构在高维情况下仍不明确，主要原因是缺乏对余正锥极端暴露射线的一般性刻画。现有维度界限不够精确，需要更精细的分析。

Method: 通过数学证明方法，针对奇数维情况建立了精确下界，针对偶数维情况推导了新的上界估计。采用理论分析而非数值实验。

Result: 对于奇数维n，证明了完全正矩阵锥最大面维度的精确下界等于n。对于偶数维n≥8，推导出该下界位于n和n+3之间的新上界估计。

Conclusion: 该研究显著改进了完全正矩阵锥最大面维度的已知界限，为理解高维情况下该锥的结构提供了更精确的理论工具。

Abstract: The structure of maximal faces of the cone of completely positive matrices is still not well understood
  in higher dimensions, mainly due to the lack of a general characterization of extreme exposed rays of the
  copositive cone beyond small matrix orders. This paper contributes to the study of maximal faces of the cone of completely positive matrices by establishing sharper bounds on their dimensions than those currently available. For every odd dimension $n$, we prove that the exact lower bound on the dimensions of maximal faces of the cone of
  $n \times n$ completely positive matrices equals $n$. For even dimensions $n \geq 8$, we derive a new upper estimate for this lower bound and show that it lies between $n$ and $n+3$. These results substantially refine the previously known bounds.

</details>


### [152] [A Globally Convergent Third-Order Newton Method via Unified Semidefinite Programming Subproblems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09682)
*Yubo Cai,Wenqi Zhu,Coralia Cartis,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: ALMTON是首个全局收敛的无正则化三阶牛顿法，采用自适应Levenberg-Marquardt二次正则化而非AR3的四次项，保持每步为可处理的SDP问题，ALMTON-Simple每迭代只需一次SDP求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三阶牛顿法（如AR3）使用四次正则化项来保证全局收敛，但这使得子问题更复杂。需要一种既能保持三阶模型优势（处理复杂优化地形），又能通过更简单的正则化机制实现全局收敛的方法。

Method: ALMTON采用自适应Levenberg-Marquardt二次正则化，保持每次迭代的立方模型。使用混合策略：当立方泰勒模型存在严格局部极小值且曲率足够时，尝试无正则化三阶步；否则激活或增加二次正则化以保证模型适定和步长可靠性。ALMTON-Simple变体每迭代只需一次SDP求解。

Result: 证明了全局收敛性和O(ε⁻²)的最坏情况评估复杂度。数值实验显示：ALMTON相比经典基线（梯度下降和阻尼牛顿）扩大了吸引域，能在二阶方法停滞的优化地形上取得进展；相比最先进的三阶实现（AR3-interp），收敛更一致且迭代次数更少。同时揭示了当前SDP求解器随维度增长带来的计算瓶颈。

Conclusion: ALMTON是首个实现全局收敛的无正则化三阶牛顿法，通过自适应二次正则化而非四次项，在保持三阶模型优势的同时提供可预测的计算成本。该方法在处理复杂非凸优化问题上具有潜力，但受限于当前SDP求解器的可扩展性。

Abstract: We propose the Adaptive Levenberg-Marquardt Third-Order Newton Method (ALMTON) for unconstrained nonconvex optimization, providing the first globally convergent realization of the unregularized third-order Newton method. Unlike the standard Adaptive Regularization framework with third-order models (AR3), which enforces global behavior through a quartic term, ALMTON employs an adaptive Levenberg-Marquardt (quadratic) regularization. This choice preserves a cubic model at every iteration, so that every subproblem is a tractable semidefinite program (SDP). In particular, the ALMTON-Simple variant requires exactly one SDP solve per iteration, making the per-iteration cost uniform and predictable.
  Algorithmically, ALMTON follows a mixed-mode strategy: it attempts an unregularized third-order step whenever the cubic Taylor model admits a strict local minimizer with adequate curvature, and activates (or increases) quadratic regularization only when needed to ensure that the model is well posed and the step is globally reliable. We establish global convergence and prove an $O(ε^{-2})$ worst-case evaluation complexity for computing an $ε$-approximate first-order stationary point. Numerical experiments show that ALMTON enlarges the basin of attraction relative to classical baselines (gradient descent and damped Newton), and can progress on landscapes where second-order methods typically stagnate. When compared with a state-of-the-art third-order implementation (AR3-interp), ALMTON converges more consistently and often in fewer iterations. We also characterize the practical scalability limits of the approach, highlighting the computational bottlenecks introduced by current SDP solvers as dimension grows.

</details>


### [153] [Long-Run Conditional Value-at-Risk Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09734)
*Qixin Wang,Hao Cao,Jian-Qiang Hu,Mingjie Hu,Li Xia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于条件风险价值(CVaR)的强化学习算法，通过单样本轨迹实现无模型的风险敏感动态优化，并证明其收敛性和最优收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融工程、能源系统和供应链管理等领域，条件风险价值(CVaR)是重要的风险度量指标。虽然马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)与长期CVaR准则能有效降低特定时间范围内的成本变异性，但传统MDP方法依赖已知的转移模型，而实际中这些模型通常不可得，因此需要无模型的风险敏感动态优化方法。

Method: 提出一种强化学习算法，基于CVaR特定的贝尔曼局部最优性方程同时进行策略评估和改进。算法采用非参数增量学习方法进行策略改进，仅依赖单一样本轨迹来识别最优策略。算法和结果进一步扩展到解决均值-CVaR优化问题。

Result: 在适当的技术条件下，证明了算法几乎必然收敛，并推导了其收敛速率。分析表明，以策略估计器的平均绝对误差衡量，最优收敛速率为O(1/n)。数值实验验证了这些结果。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种有效的无模型风险敏感强化学习算法，能够处理CVaR优化问题，具有理论保证和实际应用价值，特别是在金融工程、能源系统和供应链管理等需要风险管理的领域。

Abstract: Conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) is a prominent risk measure in financial engineering, energy systems, and supply chain management. In these domains, Markov decision processes (MDPs) with a long-run CVaR criterion effectively mitigate cost variability over a specified horizon. However, implementing MDPs relies on known transition models, which are typically unavailable in practice. This necessitates a model-free approach to risk-sensitive dynamic optimization. To tackle this challenge, we propose a reinforcement learning algorithm that simultaneously conducts policy evaluation and improvement based on a CVaR-specific Bellman local optimality equation. This algorithm employs a nonparametric incremental learning approach for policy improvement, relying on a single sample trajectory to identify the optimal policy. Under appropriate technical conditions, we prove almost sure convergence of the algorithm and derive its convergence rate. Our analysis reveals that the optimal convergence rate, measured by the mean absolute error of policy estimators, is of order O(1/n). Our main algorithm and results are further extended to solving the mean-CVaR optimization problem. Numerical experiments corroborate these results.

</details>


### [154] [Optimal Control in Age-Structured Populations: A Comparison of Rate-Control and Effort-Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09767)
*Jiguang Yu,Louis Shuo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 对比两种年龄结构种群最优收获策略：速率控制（加性收获）与努力控制（乘性收获），分析其数学和生物经济学差异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究年龄结构种群在McKendrick-von Foerster方程下的动态和最优收获，对比两种不同收获机制：速率控制（直接加性移除）和努力控制（依赖于总种群规模的乘性死亡率强度）

Method: 1. 速率控制：推导无限时域最优控制问题的Pontryagin型一阶必要最优性条件，明确描述伴随系统、横截条件和控制切换规律
2. 努力控制：分析总种群依赖如何结构性地改变最优性系统，形式化推导伴随方程中的非局部耦合项
3. 结合严格的变分控制推导和显式稳态分布分析

Result: 1. 速率控制：获得明确的伴随系统、横截条件和控制切换规律
2. 努力控制：发现总种群依赖在伴随方程中产生非局部耦合项，将所有年龄通过总存量联系起来
3. 揭示了加性和乘性收获策略在数学和生物经济学上的深刻差异

Conclusion: 加性收获（速率控制）和乘性收获（努力控制）在数学结构和最优性条件上存在本质区别，特别是努力控制引入的总种群依赖产生了伴随方程中的非局部耦合，这对年龄结构种群的最优管理策略有重要影响

Abstract: This paper investigates the dynamics and optimal harvesting of age-structured populations governed by McKendrick--von Foerster equations, contrasting two distinct harvesting mechanisms: rate-control and effort-control. For the rate-control formulation, where harvesting acts as a direct additive removal term, we derive first-order necessary optimality conditions of Pontryagin type for the associated infinite-horizon optimal control problem, explicitly characterizing the adjoint system, transversality conditions, and control switching laws. In contrast, the effort-control formulation introduces harvesting as a multiplicative mortality intensity dependent on aggregate population size. We demonstrate that this aggregate dependence structurally alters the optimality system, formally generating a nonlocal coupling term in the adjoint equation that links all ages through the total stock. By combining rigorous variational control derivations, and explicit stationary profiles, this work clarifies the profound mathematical and bioeconomic distinctions between additive and multiplicative harvesting strategies.

</details>


### [155] [Control and stabilization of cascade coupled systems: application to a 1-d heat and wave coupled system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09829)
*Lucas Davron,Pierre Lissy,Swann Marx*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究级联耦合系统（热方程与波方程耦合），分析其适定性、可控性和可稳性，利用级联结构简化证明，获得同时精确/近似可控性结果和多项式镇定


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究级联耦合系统的控制理论性质，特别是热方程与波方程的耦合系统，这类系统具有典型的级联结构（热分量影响波分量但反之不成立），需要理解其适定性、可控性和可稳性等基本控制理论问题

Method: 1. 利用级联结构简化适定性证明，避免经典能量法的繁琐计算；2. 利用级联结构证明同时精确和近似可控性；3. 通过求解Sylvester方程构造闭环控制实现多项式镇定；4. 在抽象LTI框架下讨论结果，使方法具有一般性

Result: 1. 建立了级联耦合系统的适定性；2. 证明了同时精确和近似可控性；3. 通过Sylvester方程构造的闭环控制实现了多项式镇定；4. 结果在抽象LTI框架下具有一般性，可应用于更广泛的情形

Conclusion: 级联结构为耦合系统的控制理论分析提供了简化途径，通过利用该结构成功解决了适定性、可控性和可稳性问题，特别是通过Sylvester方程构造的闭环控制实现了多项式镇定，这些结果在抽象LTI框架下具有一般适用性

Abstract: We study cascade coupled systems, for which our prototypical example is a 1-d heat equation coupled with a 1-d wave equation. The heat component is controlled through one boundary and the information is transmitted through another one to the wave component, while the wave component does not influence the heat component. Our aim is to understand the well-posedness, controllability and stabilizability properties for such a system. Establishing well-posedness is tedious using the classical energy method, which motivates us to take advantage of the cascade structure. Taking again advantage of this structure, we prove a simultaneous exact and approximate controllability result. Finally, we obtain polynomial stabilization by means of a closed-loop control defined through the solution to a Sylvester equation. These results are all discussed in an abstract LTI framework and most of our findings apply to more general situations.

</details>


### [156] [On the relations between fundamental frequency and torsional rigidity in the case of anisotropic energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09851)
*Giuseppe Buttazzo,Raul Fernandes Horta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究关于半范数H的变分能量，分析由H诱导的第一特征值和扭转刚度相关的优化问题，特别是函数F_{q,Ω}(H)=λ_H(Ω)T_H^q(Ω)在合适半范数类上的最小化和最大化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由一般半范数H定义的变分能量E_H(u)的优化问题，特别是关注由H诱导的第一特征值λ_H(Ω)和扭转刚度T_H(Ω)的乘积函数F_{q,Ω}(H)，探索在不同半范数控制下的最优性能。

Method: 在Sobolev空间H^1_0(Ω)上定义变分能量E_H(u)=1/2∫_ΩH^2(∇u)dx，其中H为一般半范数。研究由H诱导的第一特征值λ_H(Ω)和扭转刚度T_H(Ω)，构造函数F_{q,Ω}(H)=λ_H(Ω)T_H^q(Ω)，在合适的半范数类上分析该函数的极小化和极大化问题。

Result: 论文分析了函数F_{q,Ω}(H)=λ_H(Ω)T_H^q(Ω)在合适半范数类上的优化问题，包括最小化和最大化两种情况，其中q>0为固定实参数。

Conclusion: 通过研究由半范数H诱导的特征值和扭转刚度的乘积函数优化问题，揭示了半范数选择对相关变分能量性能的影响，为相关优化问题提供了理论框架和分析方法。

Abstract: We consider variational energies of the form \[E_H(u)=\frac12\int_ΩH^2(\nabla u)\,dx\] defined on the Sobolev space $H^1_0(Ω)$, where $H$ is a general seminorm. Our primary objective is to investigate optimization problems associated with the first eigenvalue $λ_H(Ω)$ and the torsional rigidity $T_H(Ω)$ induced by the seminorm $H$. In particular, we focus on functionals of the type \[F_{q,Ω}(H)=λ_H(Ω)\,T_H^q(Ω),\] where $q>0$ is a fixed real parameter. The optimization is performed with respect to the control $H$; we analyze both minimization and maximization problems for $F_{q,Ω}(H)$, as $H$ ranges over a suitable class of seminorms.

</details>


### [157] [Sparse Cuts for the Positive Semidefinite Cone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09864)
*Oktay Günlük,Paul Jünger,Jeff Linderoth,Andrea Lodi,James Luedtke*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了非凸二次优化问题，提出了一种基于稀疏线性不等式的LP松弛方法，该方法能提供与SDP松弛相同的界，并加速分支定界算法的求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸二次优化问题中，半定规划（SDP）松弛通常能提供强边界，但计算成本较高。研究旨在开发更高效的松弛方法，通过利用问题结构的稀疏性来加速全局优化。

Method: 提出使用稀疏线性不等式来外逼近半定锥，这些不等式仅涉及二次函数中出现的变量乘积对应的变量。通过求解结构化"投影"SDP的分离过程来识别这些不等式，构建稀疏LP松弛。

Result: 证明稀疏线性不等式定义的LP松弛能提供与SDP松弛相同的边界。计算研究表明，基于这些不等式的稀疏LP松弛能显著加速非凸优化问题的分支定界全局求解。

Conclusion: 稀疏线性不等式方法为处理非凸二次优化问题提供了有效的替代方案，在保持SDP松弛强边界的同时，通过LP松弛的计算优势加速全局优化算法。

Abstract: We consider optimization problems containing nonconvex quadratic functions for which semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations often yield strong bounds. We investigate linear inequalities that outer approximate the positive semidefinite cone and are sparse in the sense that they are supported only on the variables corresponding to products of variables present in quadratic functions. We show that these sparse linear inequalities yield an LP relaxation that gives the same bound as the SDP relaxation. We demonstrate how to identify these inequalities via a separation procedure that involves solving a structured ``projection'' SDP. In a computational study, we find that the sparse LP relaxations defined by these inequalities can accelerate branch-and-bound methods for globally solving nonconvex optimization problems.

</details>
